feats[:-1], dim=-1)  # (time-1, batch)\\n    delta_min = deltas.min(dim=0, keepdim=True).values\\n    delta_max = deltas.max(dim=0, keepdim=True).values\\n    delta_norm = (deltas - delta_min) / (delta_max - delta_min + 1e-8)\\n    preferred_dt = 1.0 + (eff - 1.0) * (1.0 - delta_norm)\\n    dt_indices = torch.arange(1, max_dt + 1, dtype=torch.float32, device=feats.device)\\n    sigma = 1.0\\n    log_probs = -0.5 * ((preferred_dt.unsqueeze(-1) - dt_indices) / sigma) ** 2\\n    if eff < max_dt:\\n        log_probs[..., eff:] = -1e9\\n    target_dist = F.softmax(log_probs, dim=-1)\\n    return target_dist\\n\\n\\ndef gate_loss_fn(gate_logits, target_dist):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Cross-entropy between gate predictions and supervision target.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    log_probs = F.log_softmax(gate_logits, dim=-1)\\n    return -(target_dist * log_probs).sum(dim=-1)\\n\\n\\nclass RewardEMA:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"running mean and std\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, device, alpha=1e-2):\\n        self.device = device\\n        self.alpha = alpha\\n        self.range = torch.tensor([0.05, 0.95], device=device)\\n\\n    def __call__(self, x, ema_vals):\\n        flat_x = torch.flatten(x.detach())\\n        x_quantile = torch.quantile(input=flat_x, q=self.range)\\n        # this should be in-place operation\\n        ema_vals[:] = self.alpha * x_quantile + (1 - Good, I now have a clear picture of lines 1-280 of models.py. Key observations:

1. Line 93: `max_dt` for RSSM is set to 1 when `gate_enabled=False`, but when `gate_enabled=True` it's set to the gate's max_dt
2. Line 129+: `event_gate` module is only created when `gate_enabled=True`
3. Lines 201-226: When `gate_enabled=True`, even with `gate_fixed_dt > 0`, the code still runs `tools.subsample_variable_dt()` - this is the critical point!


4. Line 213: `gate_subsample_max_dt` overrides the effective dt used for subsampling

So when `gate_fixed_dt=1` and `gate_subsample_max_dt=1`, the gate curriculum gets disabled and the effective subsampling dt is set to 1.0, which then flows through to determine the actual subsampling behavior in the subsequent lines.

Now I'm checking what happens when `subsample_variable_dt` is called with `effective_max_dt=1` and `min_dt=1` — it should only produce dt=1 samples, meaning no real subsampling occurs. But there's something crucial I'm realizing: when `gate_enabled=True`, the RSSM's `max_dt` isn't actually 1, even with `gate_fixed_dt=1`. Line 93 sets it to either 2 or `config.gate_max_dt`, so the RSSM still gets a non-zero dt_embedding dimension. This is a significant architectural difference compared to when gating is disabled.

Let me trace through the code to understand how `dt_embed_dim` behaves in RSSM when `max_dt=1`, and how the `observe` and `imagine` methods handle dt_labels.

Now I have a complete picture. Let me analyze what happens with `gate_fixed_dt=1 + gate_subsample_max_dt=1` vs the baseline (`gate_enabled=False`):

### Difference 1: RSSM architecture (dt_embedding)
- **Baseline** (`gate_enabled=False`): Line 93 → `max_dt=1` → Line 57-60 of networks.py: `max_dt <= 1` → `self._dt_embedding = None` → no embedding, `inp_dim = stoch*discrete + num_actions`


- **gate_enabled=True, gate_fixed_dt=1**: `max_dt` becomes 2 or higher depending on the gating strategy, which triggers the creation of a dt_embedding layer with 32 dimensions. This means the RSSM's GRU cell receives an additional learned 32-dimensional vector at every step, even though the actual dt value stays fixed at 1.

This is a fundamental architectural shift—the gating mechanism adds representational capacity to the recurrent core that the baseline simply doesn't have.

Now looking at the subsample_variable_dt behavior: when gate_fixed_dt=1 and gate_subsample_max_dt=1, the effective_max_dt clamps to 1, which causes subsample_variable_dt to return early without actually performing any subsampling. So despite the function being called, it exits immediately and dt_labels remains None, making this configuration functionally equivalent to the baseline in terms of subsampling behavior.

Wait, let me reconsider—the next thinking shows that dt_labels actually gets set to all 1s and is passed through to the dynamics model, which then uses the dt_embedding layer since max_dt > 1. So even though no actual subsampling occurs, the dt information is still being embedded and concatenated to the observation, which is a subtle difference from the baseline where dt_labels stays None entirely.

Now I'm looking at the event_gate module—it's created when gate_enabled is true, but the actual loss computation that would train it is skipped whenever gate_fixed_dt is greater than 0, so the gate parameters exist in the model but never get updated. Then I need to trace through the imagination path in the ImagBehavior class to see if there are other differences there.

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"# Event-Gated Adaptive-Horizon DreamerV3 — World Model & Behavior\\n# Modified from NM512/dreamerv3-torch.\\n# Changes: WorldModel integrates EventGate + variable-dt sub-sampling;\\n#          ImagBehavior uses gate-driven dt in imagination + adapted lambda-returns.\\n\\nimport copy\\nimport math\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom torch import nn\\nfrom torch.nn import functional as F\\n\\nimport networks\\nimport tools\\n\\nto_np = lambda x: x.detach().cpu().numpy()\\n\\n\\ndef compute_gate_targets(feats, max_dt, effective_max_dt=None):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Soft supervision target for the event gate derived from state deltas.\\n\\n    Large delta -> prefer small dt (index 0 = dt=1).\\n    Small delta -> prefer large dt (index K-1 = dt=K).\\n    Probability mass is placed only within [1, effective_max_dt].\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if effective_max_dt is None:\\n        effective_max_dt = max_dt\\n    eff = max(1, int(effective_max_dt))\\n    feats = feats.detach()\\n    deltas = torch.norm(feats[1:] - feats[:-1], dim=-1)  # (time-1, batch)\\n    delta_min = deltas.min(dim=0, keepdim=True).values\\n    delta_max = deltas.max(dim=0, keepdim=True).values\\n    delta_norm = (deltas - delta_min) / (delta_max - delta_min + 1e-8)\\n    preferred_dt = 1.0 + (eff - 1.0) * (1.0 - delta_norm)\\n    dt_indices = torch.arange(1, max_dt + 1, dtype=torch.float32, device=feats.device)\\n    sigma = 1.0\\n    log_probs = -0.5 * ((preferred_dt.unsqueeze(-1) - dt_indices) / sigma) ** 2\\n    if eff < max_dt:\\n        log_probs[..., eff:] = -1e9\\n    target_dist = F.softmax(log_probs, dim=-1)\\n    return target_dist\\n\\n\\ndef gate_loss_fn(gate_logits, target_dist):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Cross-entropy between gate predictions and supervision target.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    log_probs = F.log_softmax(gate_logits, dim=-1)\\n    return -(target_dist * log_probs).sum(dim=-1)\\n\\n\\nclass RewardEMA:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"running mean and std\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, device, alpha=1e-2):\\n        self.device = device\\n        self.alpha = alpha\\n        self.range = torch.tensor([0.05, 0.95], device=device)\\n\\n    def __call__(self, x, ema_vals):\\n        flat_x = torch.flatten(x.detach())\\n        x_quantile = torch.quantile(input=flat_x, q=self.range)\\n        # this should be in-place operation\\n        ema_vals[:] = self.alpha * x_quantile + (1 - self.alpha) * ema_vals\\n        scale = torch.clip(ema_vals[1] - ema_vals[0], min=1.0)\\n        offset = ema_vals[0]\\n        return offset.detach(), scale.detach()\\n\\n\\nclass WorldModel(nn.Module):\\n    def __init__(self, obs_space, act_space, step, config):\\n        super(WorldModel, self).__init__()\\n        self._step = step\\n        self._use_amp = True if config.precision == 16 else False\\n        self._config = config\\n        shapes = {k: tuple(v.shape) for k, v in obs_space.spaces.items()}\\n        self.encoder = networks.MultiEncoder(shapes, **config.encoder)\\n        self.embed_size = self.encoder.outdim\\n        self.dynamics = networks.RSSM(\\n            config.dyn_stoch,\\n            config.dyn_deter,\\n            config.dyn_hidden,\\n            config.dyn_rec_depth,\\n            config.dyn_discrete,\\n            config.act,\\n            config.norm,\\n            config.dyn_mean_act,\\n            config.dyn_std_act,\\n            config.dyn_min_std,\\n            config.unimix_ratio,\\n            config.initial,\\n            config.num_actions,\\n            self.embed_size,\\n            config.device,\\n            max_dt=(2 if getattr(config, \\\"gate_type\\\", \\\"gumbel\\\") == \\\"bernoulli_st\\\" else config.gate_max_dt) if config.gate_enabled else 1,\\n            dt_embed_dim=config.gate_dt_embed_dim,\\n        )\\n        self.heads = nn.ModuleDict()\\n        if config.dyn_discrete:\\n            feat_size = config.dyn_stoch * config.dyn_discrete + config.dyn_deter\\n        else:\\n            feat_size = config.dyn_stoch + config.dyn_deter\\n        self.heads[\\\"decoder\\\"] = networks.MultiDecoder(\\n            feat_size, shapes, **config.decoder\\n        )\\n        self.heads[\\\"reward\\\"] = networks.MLP(\\n            feat_size,\\n            (255,) if config.reward_head[\\\"dist\\\"] == \\\"symlog_disc\\\" else (),\\n            config.reward_h\\n\\n... self.value.parameters()))\\n        return imag_feat, imag_state, imag_action, weights, metrics\\n\\n    def _imagine(self, start, policy, horizon):\\n        dynamics = self._world_model.dynamics\\n        flatten = lambda x: x.reshape([-1] + list(x.shape[2:]))\\n        start = {k: flatten(v) for k, v in start.items()}\\n\\n        if self._config.gate_enabled:\\n            gate = self._world_model.event_gate\\n            config = self._config\\n            step = self._world_model._step\\n\\n            effective_max_dt = self._world_model._effective_max_dt\\n\\n            gate_active = (\\n                gate is not None and self._world_model._gate_scale > 0\\n                and config.gate_fixed_dt == 0\\n            )\\n\\n            def step_fn(prev, _):\\n                state, _, _, prev_dt = prev\\n                feat = dynamics.get_feat(state)\\n                inp = feat.detach()\\n                action = policy(inp).sample()\\n                succ = dynamics.img_step(state, action, dt=prev_dt)\\n                succ_feat = dynamics.get_feat(succ)\\n                if gate_active:\\n                    _, next_dt, _ = gate(succ_feat.detach(), step, hard=True, effective_max_dt=effective_max_dt)\\n                    next_dt = torch.clamp(next_dt, 1, max(1, int(effective_max_dt)))\\n                else:\\n                    batch_size = succ_feat.shape[0]\\n                    fixed = config.gate_fixed_dt if config.gate_fixed_dt > 0 else 1\\n                    next_dt = torch.full(\\n                        (batch_size,), fixed, dtype=torch.long, device=succ_feat.device\\n                    )\\n                return succ, feat, action, next_dt\\n\\n            init_dt = torch.ones(\\n                start[list(start.keys())[0]].shape[0],\\n                dtype=torch.long, device=config.device,\\n            )\\n            if config.gate_fixed_dt > 0:\\n                init_dt = init_dt * config.gate_fixed_dt\\n            succ, feats, actions, dts = tools.static_scan(\\n                step_fn, [torch.arange(horizon)],\\n                (start, None, None, init_dt),\\n            )\\n            states = {\\n                k: torch.cat([start[k][None], v[:-1]], 0)\\n                for k, v in succ.items()\\n            }\\n            return feats, states, actions, dts\\n        else:\\n            def step(prev, _):\\n                state, _, _ = prev\\n                feat = dynamics.get_feat(state)\\n                inp = feat.detach()\\n                action = policy(inp).sample()\\n                succ = dynamics.img_step(state, action)\\n                return succ, feat, action\\n\\n            succ, feats, actions = tools.static_scan(\\n                step, [torch.arange(horizon)], (start, None, None)\\n            )\\n            states = {\\n                k: torch.cat([start[k][None], v[:-1]], 0)\\n                for k, v in succ.items()\\n            }\\n            return feats, states, actions\\n\\n    def _compute_target(self, imag_feat, imag_state, reward, dts=None, init_dt=1.0):\\n        if \\\"cont\\\" in self._world_model.heads:\\n            inp = self._world_model.dynamics.get_feat(imag_state)\\n            discount = self._config.discount * self._world_model.heads[\\\"cont\\\"](inp).mean\\n        else:\\n            discount        init_dt_tensor = torch.full_like(dts[:1], init_dt)\\n\\n            horizon_mask = None\\n            if getattr(self._config, 'gate_type', 'gumbel') == 'bernoulli_st':\\n                phys_horizon = getattr(self._config, 'bernoulli_physical_horizon', 15)\\n                batch_size = dts.shape[1]\\n                trans_dts_h = torch.cat([\\n                    torch.full((1, feats[:-1], dim=-1)  # (time-1, batch)\\n    delta_min = deltas.min(dim=0, keepdim=True).values\\n    delta_max = deltas.max(dim=0, keepdim=True).values\\n    delta_norm = (deltas - delta_min) / (delta_max - delta_min + 1e-8)\\n    preferred_dt = 1.0 + (eff - 1.0) * (1.0 - delta_norm)\\n    dt_indices = torch.arange(1, max_dt + 1, dtype=torch.float32, device=feats.device)\\n    sigma = 1.0\\n    log_probs = -0.5 * ((preferred_dt.unsqueeze(-1) - dt_indices) / sigma) ** 2\\n    if eff < max_dt:\\n        log_probs[..., eff:] = -1e9\\n    target_dist = F.softmax(log_probs, dim=-1)\\n    return target_dist\\n\\n\\ndef gate_loss_fn(gate_logits, target_dist):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Cross-entropy between gate predictions and supervision target.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    log_probs = F.log_softmax(gate_logits, dim=-1)\\n    return -(target_dist * log_probs).sum(dim=-1)\\n\\n\\nclass RewardEMA:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"running mean and std\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, device, alpha=1e-2):\\n        self.device = device\\n        self.alpha = alpha\\n        self.range = torch.tensor([0.05, 0.95], device=device)\\n\\n    def __call__(self, x, ema_vals):\\n        flat_x = torch.flatten(x.detach())\\n        x_quantile = torch.quantile(input=flat_x, q=self.range)\\n        # this should be in-place operation\\n        ema_vals[:] = self.alpha * x_quantile + (1 - self.alpha) * ema_vals\\n        scale = torch.clip(ema_vals[1] - ema_vals[0], min=1.0)\\n        offset = ema_vals[0]\\n        return offset.detach(), scale.detach()\\n\\n\\nclass WorldModel(nn.Module):\\n    def __init__(self, obs_space, act_space, step, config):\\n        super(WorldModel, self).__init__()\\n        self._step = step\\n        self._use_amp = True if config.precision == 16 else False\\n        self._config = config\\n        shapes = {k: tuple(v.shape) for k, v in obs_space.spaces.items()}\\n        self.encoder = networks.MultiEncoder(shapes, **config.encoder)\\n        self.embed_size = self.encoder.outdim\\n        self.dynamics = networks.RSSM(\\n            config.dyn_stoch,\\n            config.dyn_deter,\\n            config.dyn_hidden,\\n            config.dyn_rec_depth,\\n            config.dyn_discrete,\\n            config.act,\\n            config.norm,\\n            config.dyn_mean_act,\\n            config.dyn_std_act,\\n            config.dyn_min_std,\\n            config.unimix_ratio,\\n            config.initial,\\n            config.num_actions,\\n            self.embed_size,\\n            config.device,\\n            max_dt=(2 if getattr(config, \\\"gate_type\\\", \\\"gumbel\\\") == \\\"bernoulli_st\\\" else config.gate_max_dt) if config.gate_enabled else 1,\\n            dt_embed_dim=config.gate_dt_embed_dim,\\n        )\\n        self.heads = nn.ModuleDict()\\n        if config.dyn_discrete:\\n            feat_size = config.dyn_stoch * config.dyn_discrete + config.dyn_deter\\n        else:\\n            feat_size = config.dyn_stoch + config.dyn_deter\\n        self.heads[\\\"decoder\\\"] = networks.MultiDecoder(\\n            feat_size, shapes, **config.decoder\\n        )\\n        self.heads[\\\"reward\\\"] = networks.MLP(\\n            feat_size,\\n            (255,) if config.reward_head[\\\"dist\\\"] == \\\"symlog_disc\\\" else (),\\n            config.reward_h\\n\\n... dt_counts = torch.bincount(\\n                dts.reshape(-1).long(),\\n                minlength=eff_max_dt + 1,\\n            )[1:]\\n            dt_dist = dt_counts.float() / dt_counts.sum()\\n            for k in range(eff_max_dt):\\n                metrics[f\\\"gate_dt_{k+1}_frac\\\"] = to_np(dt_dist[k])\\n            emp_entropy = -(dt_dist * torch.log(dt_dist + 1e-8)).sum()\\n            metrics[\\\"gate_empirical_entropy\\\"] = to_np(emp_entropy)\\n            eff_dt = max(1, int(self._world_model._effective_max_dt))\\n            max_entropy = math.log(eff_dt) if eff_dt > 1 else 1.0\\n            metrics[\\\"gate_entropy_ratio\\\"] = to_np(emp_entropy) / max_entropy\\n\\n            if getattr(self._config, 'gate_type', 'gumbel') == 'bernoulli_st':\\n                phys_h = getattr(self._config, 'bernoulli_physical_horizon', 15)\\n                init_dt_f = float(self._config.gate_fixed_dt) if self._config.gate_fixed_dt > 0 else 1.0\\n                bsz = dts_float.shape[1]\\n                tr_h = torch.cat([\\n                    torch.full((1, bsz), init_dt_f, device=dts.device),\\n                    dts_float[:dts_float.shape[0]-1]\\n {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"# Event-Gated Adaptive-Horizon DreamerV3 — World Model & Behavior\\n# Modified from NM512/dreamerv3-torch.\\n# Changes: WorldModel integrates EventGate + variable-dt sub-sampling;\\n#          ImagBehavior uses gate-driven dt in imagination + adapted lambda-returns.\\n\\nimport copy\\nimport math\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom torch import nn\\nfrom torch.nn import functional as F\\n\\nimport networks\\nimport tools\\n\\nto_np = lambda x: x.detach().cpu().numpy()\\n\\n\\ndef compute_gate_targets(feats, max_dt, effective_max_dt=None):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Soft supervision target for the event gate derived from state deltas.\\n\\n    Large delta -> prefer small dt (index 0 = dt=1).\\n    Small delta -> prefer large dt (index K-1 = dt=K).\\n    Probability mass is placed only within [1, effective_max_dt].\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if effective_max_dt is None:\\n        effective_max_dt = max_dt\\n    eff = max(1, int(effective_max_dt))\\n    feats = feats.detach()\\n    deltas = torch.norm(feats[1:] - feats[:-1], dim=-1)  # (time-1, batch)\\n    delta_min = deltas.min(dim=0, keepdim=True).values\\n    delta_max = deltas.max(dim=0, keepdim=True).values\\n    delta_norm = (deltas - delta_min) / (delta_max - feats[:-1], dim=-1)  # (time-1, batch)\\n    delta_min = deltas.min(dim=0, keepdim=True).values\\n    delta_max = deltas.max(dim=0, keepdim=True).values\\n    delta_norm = (deltas - delta_min) / (delta_max - register ema_vals to nn.Module for enabling torch.save and torch.load\\n            self.register_buffer(\\n                \\\"ema_vals\\\", torch.zeros((2,), device=self._config.device)\\n            )\\n            self.reward_ema = RewardEMA(device=self._config.device)\\n\\n    def _train(\\n        self,\\n        start,\\n        objective,\\n    ):\\n        self._update_slow_target()\\n        metrics = {}\\n\\n        with tools.RequiresGrad(self.actor):\\n            with torch.cuda.amp.autocast(self._use_amp):\\n                if self._config.gate_enabled:\\n                    imag_feat, imag_state, imag_action, dts = self._imagine(\\n                        start, self.actor, self._config.imag_horizon\\n                    )\\n                else:\\n                    imag_feat, imag_state, imag_action = self._imagine(\\n                        start, self.actor, self._config.imag_horizon\\n                    )\\n                    dts = None\\n                reward = objective(imag_feat, imag_state, imag_action)\\n                actor_ent = self.actor(imag_feat).entropy()\\n                state_ent = self._world_model.dynamics.get_dist(imag_state).entropy()\\n                # (time, batch, 1), (time, batch, 1) -> (1,)\\\\n                value_loss = torch.mean(weights[:-1] * value_loss[:, :, None])\\\\n\\\\n        metrics.update(tools.tensorstats(value.mode(), \\\\\\\"value\\\\\\\"))\\\\n        metrics.update(tools.tensorstats(target, \\\\\\\"target\\\\\\\"))\\\\n        metrics.update(tools.tensorstats(reward, \\\\\\\"imag_reward\\\\\\\"))\\\\n        if self._config.actor[\\\\\\\"dist\\\\\\\"] in [\\\\\\\"onehot\\\\\\\"]:\\\\n            metrics.update(\\\\n                tools.tensorstats(\\\\n                    torch.argmax(imag_action, dim=-1).float(), \\\\\\\"imag_action\\\\\\\"\\\\n                )\\\\n            )\\\\n        else:\\\\n            metrics.update(tools.tensorstats(imag_action, \\\\\\\"imag_action\\\\\\\"))\\\\n        metrics[\\\\\\\"actor_entropy\\\\\\\"] = to_np(torch.mean(actor_ent))\\\\n\\\\n        if self._config.gate_enabled and dts is not None:\\\\n            dts_float = dts.float()\\\\n            metrics[\\\\\\\"gate_dt_mean\\\\\\\"] = to_np(torch.mean(dts_float))\\\\n            metrics[\\\\\\\"gate_dt_std\\\\\\\"] = to_np(torch.std(dts_float))\\\\n            metrics[\\\\\\\"gate_dt_min\\\\\\\"] = to_np(torch.min(dts_float))\\\\n            metrics[\\\\\\\"gate_dt_max\\\\\\\"] = to_np(torch.max(dts_float))\\\\n            eff_max_dt = 2 if getattr(self._config, 'gate_type', 'gumbel') == 'bernoulli_st' else self._config.gate_max_dt\\\\n            dt_counts = torch.bincount(\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.75}}\"}",
    "structuredContent": {
      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"# Event-Gated Adaptive-Horizon DreamerV3 — World Model & Behavior\\n# Modified from NM512/dreamerv3-torch.\\n# Changes: WorldModel integrates EventGate + variable-dt sub-sampling;\\n#          ImagBehavior uses gate-driven dt in imagination + adapted lambda-returns.\\n\\nimport copy\\nimport math\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom torch import nn\\nfrom torch.nn import functional as F\\n\\nimport networks\\nimport tools\\n\\nto_np = lambda x: x.detach().cpu().numpy()\\n\\n\\ndef compute_gate_targets(feats, max_dt, effective_max_dt=None):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Soft supervision target for the event gate derived from state deltas.\\n\\n    Large delta -> prefer small dt (index 0 = dt=1).\\n    Small delta -> prefer large dt (index K-1 = dt=K).\\n    Probability mass is placed only within [1, effective_max_dt].\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if effective_max_dt is None:\\n        effective_max_dt = max_dt\\n    eff = max(1, int(effective_max_dt))\\n    feats = feats.detach()\\n    deltas = torch.norm(feats[1:] - feats[:-1], dim=-1)  # (time-1, batch)\\n    delta_min = deltas.min(dim=0, keepdim=True).values\\n    delta_max = deltas.max(dim=0, keepdim=True).values\\n    delta_norm = (deltas - delta_min) / (delta_max - delta_min + 1e-8)\\n    preferred_dt = 1.0 + (eff - 1.0) * (1.0 - delta_norm)\\n    dt_indices = torch.arange(1, max_dt + 1, dtype=torch.float32, device=feats.device)\\n    sigma = 1.0\\n    log_probs = -0.5 * ((preferred_dt.unsqueeze(-1) - dt_indices) / sigma) ** 2\\n    if eff < max_dt:\\n        log_probs[..., eff:] = -1e9\\n    target_dist = F.softmax(log_probs, dim=-1)\\n    return target_dist\\n\\n\\ndef gate_loss_fn(gate_logits, target_dist):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Cross-entropy between gate predictions and supervision target.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    log_probs = F.log_softmax(gate_logits, dim=-1)\\n    return -(target_dist * log_probs).sum(dim=-1)\\n\\n\\nclass RewardEMA:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"running mean and std\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, device, alpha=1e-2):\\n        self.device = device\\n        self.alpha = alpha\\n        self.range = torch.tensor([0.05, 0.95], device=device)\\n\\n    def __call__(self, x, ema_vals):\\n        flat_x = torch.flatten(x.detach())\\n        x_quantile = torch.quantile(input=flat_x, q=self.range)\\n        # this should be in-place operation\\n        ema_vals[:] = self.alpha * x_quantile + (1 - self.alpha) * ema_vals\\n        scale = torch.clip(ema_vals[1] - ema_vals[0], min=1.0)\\n        offset = ema_vals[0]\\n        return offset.detach(), scale.detach()\\n\\n\\nclass WorldModel(nn.Module):\\n    def __init__(self, obs_space, act_space, step, config):\\n        super(WorldModel, self).__init__()\\n        self._step = step\\n        self._use_amp = True if config.precision == 16 else False\\n        self._config = config\\n        shapes = {k: tuple(v.shape) for k, v in obs_space.spaces.items()}\\n        self.encoder = networks.MultiEncoder(shapes, **config.encoder)\\n        self.embed_size = self.encoder.outdim\\n        self.dynamics = networks.RSSM(\\n            config.dyn_stoch,\\n            config.dyn_deter,\\n            config.dyn_hidden,\\n            config.dyn_rec_depth,\\n            config.dyn_discrete,\\n            config.act,\\n            config.norm,\\n            config.dyn_mean_act,\\n            config.dyn_std_act,\\n            config.dyn_min_std,\\n            config.unimix_ratio,\\n            config.initial,\\n            config.num_actions,\\n            self.embed_size,\\n            config.device,\\n            max_dt=(2 if getattr(config, \\\"gate_type\\\", \\\"gumbel\\\") == \\\"bernoulli_st\\\" else config.gate_max_dt) if config.gate_enabled else 1,\\n            dt_embed_dim=config.gate_dt_embed_dim,\\n        )\\n        self.heads = nn.ModuleDict()\\n        if config.dyn_discrete:\\n            feat_size = config.dyn_stoch * config.dyn_discrete + config.dyn_deter\\n        else:\\n            feat_size = config.dyn_stoch + config.dyn_deter\\n        self.heads[\\\"decoder\\\"] = networks.MultiDecoder(\\n            feat_size, shapes, **config.decoder\\n        )\\n        self.heads[\\\"reward\\\"] = networks.MLP(\\n            feat_size,\\n            (255,) if config.reward_head[\\\"dist\\\"] == \\\"symlog_disc\\\" else (),\\n            config.reward_h\\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 15315 字符] ...\\n\\nnfig.critic[\\\"lr\\\"],\\n            config.critic[\\\"eps\\\"],\\n            config.critic[\\\"grad_clip\\\"],\\n            **kw,\\n        )\\n        print(\\n            f\\\"Optimizer value_opt has {sum(param.numel() for param in self.value.parameters())} variables.\\\"\\n        )\\n        if self._config.reward_EMA:\\n            # register ema_vals to nn.Module for enabling torch.save and torch.load\\n            self.register_buffer(\\n                \\\"ema_vals\\\", torch.zeros((2,), device=self._config.device)\\n            )\\n            self.reward_ema = RewardEMA(device=self._config.device)\\n\\n    def _train(\\n        self,\\n        start,\\n        objective,\\n    ):\\n        self._update_slow_target()\\n        metrics = {}\\n\\n        with tools.RequiresGrad(self.actor):\\n            with torch.cuda.amp.autocast(self._use_amp):\\n                if self._config.gate_enabled:\\n                    imag_feat, imag_state, imag_action, dts = self._imagine(\\n                        start, self.actor, self._config.imag_horizon\\n                    )\\n                else:\\n                    imag_feat, imag_state, imag_action = self._imagine(\\n                        start, self.actor, self._config.imag_horizon\\n                    )\\n                    dts = None\\n                reward = objective(imag_feat, imag_state, imag_action)\\n                actor_ent = self.actor(imag_feat).entropy()\\n                state_ent = self._world_model.dynamics.get_dist(imag_state).entropy()\\n                # this target is not scaled by ema or sym_log.\\n                init_dt_val = float(self._config.gate_fixed_dt) if self._config.gate_fixed_dt > 0 else 1.0\\n                target, weights, base = self._compute_target(\\n                    imag_feat, imag_state, reward, dts=dts, init_dt=init_dt_val,\\n                )\\n                actor_loss, mets = self._compute_actor_loss(\\n                    imag_feat,\\n                    imag_action,\\n                    target,\\n                    weights,\\n                    base,\\n                )\\n                actor_loss -= self._config.actor[\\\"entropy\\\"] * actor_ent[:-1, ..., None]\\n                actor_loss = torch.mean(actor_loss)\\n                metrics.update(mets)\\n                value_input = imag_feat\\n\\n        with tools.RequiresGrad(self.value):\\n            with torch.cuda.amp.autocast(self._use_amp):\\n                value = self.value(value_input[:-1].detach())\\n                target = torch.stack(target, dim=1)\\n                # (time, batch, 1), (time, batch, 1) -> (time, batch)\\n                value_loss = -value.log_prob(target.detach())\\n                slow_target = self._slow_value(value_input[:-1].detach())\\n                if self._config.critic[\\\"slow_target\\\"]:\\n                    value_loss -= value.log_prob(slow_target.mode().detach())\\n                # (time, batch, 1), (time, batch, 1) -> (1,)\\n                value_loss = torch.mean(weights[:-1] * value_loss[:, :, None])\\n\\n        metrics.update(tools.tensorstats(value.mode(), \\\"value\\\"))\\n        metrics.update(tools.tensorstats(target, \\\"target\\\"))\\n        metrics.update(tools.tensorstats(reward, \\\"imag_reward\\\"))\\n        if self._config.actor[\\\"dist\\\"] in [\\\"onehot\\\"]:\\n            metrics.update(\\n                tools.tensorstats(\\n                    torch.argmax(imag_action, dim=-1).float(), \\\"imag_action\\\"\\n                )\\n            )\\n        else:\\n            metrics.update(tools.tensorstats(imag_action, \\\"imag_action\\\"))\\n        metrics[\\\"actor_entropy\\\"] = to_np(torch.mean(actor_ent))\\n\\n        if self._config.gate_enabled and dts is not None:\\n            dts_float = dts.float()\\n            metrics[\\\"gate_dt_mean\\\"] = to_np(torch.mean(dts_float))\\n            metrics[\\\"gate_dt_std\\\"] = to_np(torch.std(dts_float))\\n            metrics[\\\"gate_dt_min\\\"] = to_np(torch.min(dts_float))\\n            metrics[\\\"gate_dt_max\\\"] = to_np(torch.max(dts_float))\\n            eff_max_dt = 2 if getattr(self._config, 'gate_type', 'gumbel') == 'bernoulli_st' else self._config.gate_max_dt\\n            dt_counts = torch.bincount(\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.75}}"

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"import argparse\\nimport functools\\nimport os\\nimport pathlib\\nimport sys\\n\\nos.environ[\\\"MUJOCO_GL\\\"] = \\\"osmesa\\\"\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport ruamel.yaml as yaml\\n\\nsys.path.append(str(pathlib.Path(__file__).parent))\\n\\nimport exploration as expl\\nimport models\\nimport tools\\nimport envs.wrappers as wrappers\\nfrom parallel import Parallel, Damy\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom torch import nn\\nfrom torch import distributions as torchd\\n\\n\\nto_np = lambda x: x.detach().cpu().numpy()\\n\\n\\nclass Dreamer(nn.Module):\\n    def __init__(self, obs_space, act_space, config, logger, dataset):\\n        super(Dreamer, self).__init__()\\n        self._config = config\\n        self._logger = logger\\n        self._should_log = tools.Every(config.log_every)\\n        batch_steps = config.batch_size * config.batch_length\\n        self._should_train = tools.Every(batch_steps / config.train_ratio)\\n        self._should_pretrain = tools.Once()\\n        self._should_reset = tools.Every(config.reset_every)\\n        self._should_expl = tools.Until(int(config.expl_until / config.action_repeat))\\n        self._metrics = {}\\n        # this is update step\\n        self._step = logger.step // config.action_repeat\\n        self._update_count = 0\\n        self._dataset = dataset\\n        self._wm = models.WorldModel(obs_space, act_space, self._step, config)\\n        self._task_behavior = models.ImagBehavior(config, self._wm)\\n        if (\\n            config.compile and os.name != \\\"nt\\\"\\n        ):  # compilation is not supported on windows\\n            self._wm = torch.compile(self._wm)\\n            self._task_behavior = torch.compile(self._task_behavior)\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.35}}"}

      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"import argparse\\nimport functools\\nimport os\\nimport pathlib\\nimport sys\\n\\nos.environ[\\\"MUJOCO_GL\\\"] = \\\"osmesa\\\"\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport ruamel.yaml as yaml\\n\\nsys.path.append(str(pathlib.Path(__file__).parent))\\n\\nimport exploration as expl\\nimport models\\nimport tools\\nimport envs.wrappers as wrappers\\nfrom parallel import Parallel, Damy\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom torch import nn\\nfrom torch import distributions as torchd\\n\\n\\nto_np = lambda x: x.detach().cpu().numpy()\\n\\n\\nclass Dreamer(nn.Module):\\n    def __init__(self, obs_space, act_space, config, logger, dataset):\\n        super(Dreamer, self).__init__()\\n        self._config = config\\n        self._logger = logger\\n        self._should_log = tools.Every(config.log_every)\\n        batch_steps = config.batch_size * config.batch_length\\n        self._should_train = tools.Every(batch_steps / config.train_ratio)\\n        self._should_pretrain = tools.Once()\\n        self._should_reset = tools.Every(config.reset_every)\\n        self._should_expl = tools.Until(int(config.expl_until / config.action_repeat))\\n        self._metrics = {}\\n        # this is update step\\n        self._step = logger.step // config.action_repeat\\n        self._update_count = 0\\n        self._dataset = dataset\\n        self._wm = models.WorldModel(obs_space, act_space, self._step, config)\\n        self._task_behavior = models.ImagBehavior(config, self._wm)\\n        if (\\n            config.compile and os.name != \\\"nt\\\"\\n        ):  # compilation is not supported on windows\\n            self._wm = torch.compile(self._wm)\\n            self._task_behavior = torch.compile(self._task_behavior)\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.35}}"

## Analysis: `gate_enabled=True, gate_fixed_dt=1, gate_subsample_max_dt=1` vs `gate_enabled=False` (baseline)

### Difference 1: RSSM dt_embedding (CRITICAL)
- **Baseline** (`gate_enabled=False`): RSSM constructed with `max_dt=1` → `_dt_embedding = None` → GRU input = `stoch*discrete + num_actions`


- **gate_enabled=True, gate_fixed_dt=1**: RSSM constructed with `max_dt=gate_max_dt` (default 8) or `max_dt=2` (bernoulli_st) → `_dt_embedding = nn.Embedding(max_dt, 32)` → GRU input = `stoch*discrete + num_actions + 32`

Even when dt is fixed at 1, the GRU still receives an additional 32-dimensional embedding of that dt value, fundamentally altering the model's input dimensionality and architecture.

### Difference 2: Event gate module (minor)
- **Baseline**: no event_gate module
- **gate_enabled=True**: event_gate module exists, but with `gate_fixed`

_dt > 0`, the gate loss isn't computed (only when `gate_fixed_dt == 0`), so the gate parameters never receive gradient updates. It's dead weight that shouldn't meaningfully impact training.

### Difference 3: subsample_variable_dt called (NEUTRAL when dt=1)
- **Baseline**: `dt_labels = None`, subsample_variable_dt not called
- **gate_enabled=True, subsample_max_dt=1**: subsample_variable_dt called with `effective_max_dt=1` → early return leaves `data` unchanged, `dt_labels = ones`

The data path is identical, but `dt_labels` gets passed to `dynamics.observe()`, which routes dt=1 through the embedding layer as described above.

### Difference 4: Imagination path
- **Baseline**: `_imagine` returns `(feats, states, actions`

Continuing with the imagination path, when gate is enabled with fixed dt=1, the return includes `dts` all set to 1, which then gets passed to `img_step` and concatenates the dt embedding. The target computation switches to `lambda_return_variable_dt`, but since dt is always 1, the discount adjustment `pcont ** 1.0` equals `pcont`, making it mathematically equivalent to the baseline `lambda_return`.

### Difference 5: Bernoulli-specific horizon masking
- If `gate_type == 'bernoulli_st'`, there's a `bernoulli_physical_horizon` masking applied in `_compute_target`. With dt locked at 1, this masking becomes a no-op.
- The gumbel gate type (default) doesn't activate this masking at all, so it's not a factor for the standard configuration.

Even with `gate_enabled=True, gate_fixed_dt=1, gate_subsample_max_dt=1`, the dt embedding still gets concatenated, which breaks equivalence with the baseline. The true baseline is `gate_enabled=False`, which completely bypasses all gate logic.

### Assistant

_source line 146_

从 008a 的 metrics 确认：gate_type 是 gumbel（非 bernoulli_st），因为有 8 个 dt_frac 指标。现在我有完整分析。

## 代码差异分析

`gate_enabled=True, gate_fixed_dt=1, gate_subsample_max_dt=1` vs `gate_enabled=False` (baseline) 的差异：

**差异 1 (关键): RSSM dt_embedding**
- Baseline: `max_dt=1` → `_dt_embedding=None` → GRU input = `stoch*32 + actions`
- 008e config: `max_dt=8` (gumbel, gate_max_dt=8) → `_dt_embedding=Embedding(8, 32)` → GRU input 额外 +32维
- dt=1 时永远使用 `embedding[0]`，是一个学习到的常量向量。改变了模型架构。

**差异 2 (次要): event_gate 模块存在**
- gate_fixed_dt>0 时 gate loss 不计算（条件 `gate_fixed_dt==0` 不满足），gate 参数不接收梯度。纯死参数。

**差异 3 (数学等价): lambda_return 路径**
- Baseline: `tools.lambda_return()`
- 008e: `tools.lambda_return_variable_dt()` with dt=1 → `pcont^1 = pcont`，数学上完全等价。

**差异 4 (无影响): subsample_variable_dt**
- 008e 调用 `subsample_variable_dt(effective_max_dt=1)` → 早返回，data 不变，dt_labels 全 1。

**结论：不等价。** 差异 1 的 dt_embedding 改变了 RSSM 的输入维度（+32维常量），虽然信息量为零（始终同一个 embedding），但会影响 GRU 动态。

## 产出汇总

### 1. 代码差异分析

| 差异点 | 详情 | 影响 |
|--------|------|------|
| **RSSM dt_embedding** | `gate_enabled=True` → RSSM `max_dt=8` → `Embedding(8,32)` 注入 GRU 输入，+32维 | **结构性差异**。dt=1 时是常量向量，但改变了 GRU 输入维度 |
| event_gate 模块 | 存在但 `gate_fixed_dt>0` 时不训练（无梯度） | 死参数，不影响训练动态 |
| lambda_return 路径 | 使用 `lambda_return_variable_dt(dt=1)` → `pcont^1 = pcont` | **数学等价**，无影响 |
| subsample_variable_dt | `effective_max_dt=1` 早返回，data 不变 | **无影响**，dt_labels 全 1 |

**结论**：`gate_enabled=True, gate_fixed_dt=1, gate_subsample_max_dt=1` **不完全等价于** baseline（`gate_enabled=False`），唯一实质差异是 RSSM 的 dt_embedding(0) 常量向量。但这个差异对训练效果的影响正是 008e 要验证的。

### 2. 008e 完整启动命令

与 008a/c/d 保持一致的参数模板（`--configs defaults dmc_proprio`, `--steps 105000`, `--seed 1`, `--compile False`）：

```bash
cd /root/autodl-tmp/event-gated-adaptive-horizon && \
source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && \
export LD_PRELOAD=/usr/lib/x86_64-linux-gnu/libstdc++.so.6 && \
export MUJOCO_GL=osmesa && export PYOPENGL_PLATFORM=osmesa && \
CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=&lt;GPU> python dreamer.py \
  --configs defaults dmc_proprio \
  --task dmc_walker_walk \
  --logdir logdir/exp_008e_dt1_no_subsample_h15 \
  --steps 105000 --seed 1 --compile False --device cuda:0 \
  --gate_enabled True --gate_fixed_dt 1 --gate_subsample_max_dt 1 \
  --imag_horizon 15
```

对比 008a 命令的唯一差异：`--gate_fixed_dt 2` → `--gate_fixed_dt 1`

### 3. Dry-run 验证结果

- 配置解析正确，模型初始化成功
- 训练循环正常运行，metrics 正常写入
- gate metrics 正确：`gate_dt_mean=1.0`, `gate_dt_1_frac=1.0`
- 无 crash、无 error

### 实验解读预期

- 008e ≈ baseline（~400）→ gate 基础设施 + dt_embedding(0) 常量对训练无影响，008a 的提升来自 dt=2 imagination
- 008e ≫ baseline → dt_embedding 本身有正面效果（额外 32 维表示容量），需要进一步隔离
- 008e ≪ baseline → gate 代码路径引入了意外负面影响
